Objective and gradient:
Let a nonnegative length n. We optimize f(a)= 2 n max(c)/S^2. Scale invariant. We can normalize S=n after each step. Keep a in [0,1000].
Use LSE: L = cmax + (1/β) log ∑ exp(β(c-cmax)). p=exp(β(c-cmax))/sum. Then dL/da_i = ∑ p_k dc_k/da_i. dc_k/da_i = 2 a_{k-i} if 0≤k-i<n else 0.
Then grad = 2n/S^2 * h - 4n L/S^3 * S? Wait dF/da = 2n/S^2 * dL - 4n L/S^3 * e_i (where S=sum a). So grad_i = (2n/S^2) h_i - (4n L/S^3). But this assumes S fixed? Actually derivative of F(c(a),S(a)) exactly. Good. But if we enforce normalization by scaling after step, gradient of normalized objective not exactly; okay.
h_i = ∑_k p_k 2 a_{k-i} valid = 2 b_i with b_i = ∑_{j=0}^{n-1} p_{i+j} a_j. So h_i=2*b_i.
Compute b_i efficiently with convolution as above.
Let's derive exact FFT implementation:
Input a length n, p length m=2n-1.

In Python: conv[M-n+1:M+1][::-1]. Need M-n+1 = (2n-2)-(n-1)=n-1. So indices n-1..2n-2 inclusive length n. b = conv[n-1:2n-1][::-1]. Wait conv indices n-1 to 2n-2. For i=0 t=M=2n-2; for i=n-1 t=n-1. Then h=2*b.

Let's implement `eval_transfer(a, c, S, i, j, frac)`:
```
def test_transfer(a, c, S, i, j, frac):
    old_i=a[i]; delta=frac*old_i
    if delta<=0: return current
    a2 = a.copy(); c2 = c.copy(); S2=S
    # apply i decrease
    d1=-delta
    temp = a2[i]
    # To compute inc for d1, use old a2 (a2[i] still old)
    inc1 = 2*d1 * a2  # uses old_i
    if i < n: inc1[i] += d1*d1
    c2[i:i+n] += inc1
    a2[i] = temp + d1
    S2 += d1
    # apply j increase
    d2=delta
    inc2 = 2*d2 * a2  # a2 now has new i, still old j
    if j < n: inc2[j] += d2*d2
    c2[j:j+n] += inc2
    a2[j] += d2
    # S2 unchanged (d1+d2=0)
    newmax = c2.max()
    return 2*n*newmax/(S2*S2)
```
But `inc2` uses `a2` length n; it includes updated a_i, and a_j old. Good. Cross terms correct. However for two coordinate update, the affected segment for j uses a2 with a_i updated. Now, let's refine numeric functions.

`next_pow2(m)`:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```

`self_conv(a)`:
```
def self_conv(a):
    n = len(a)
    L = next_pow2(2*n - 1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A*A, L)[:2*n-1]
    # c might have tiny negative; clip
    if c.min() < 0:
       c = np.maximum(c, 0)
    return c
```
Clipping may introduce small inexact but okay. For coordinate updates, if we clip c after self_conv, then incremental updates using c may not match exact due to clipped negatives; small. Could not clip and just max (negative small < max). For score, max ignores small negative. For p, negative small okay maybe exp lower; but c-cmax. To be safe, `c = np.where(c < 0 & >-1e-9, 0, c)`. Use `np.maximum(c,0)`. This is fine; for incremental updates, if we update c from clipped, adding increments may become exact+clip? If initial clipped negatives to zero, subsequent updates maybe not exact for negative lags but negatives irrelevant. Could accumulate errors but small. We can recompute c from scratch periodically.

`weighted_conv(a,p)`:
Inputs a length n, p length 2n-1. Need b_i = ∑_{j} p_{i+j} a_j.
Use `a_rev = a[::-1]`, L=next_pow2(3*n-2) (length? For convolution of lengths n and m (m=2n-1): n+m-1=3n-2. The max index =3n-3, need output length 3n-2. Next pow2 >=3n-2.)
```
A=np.fft.rfft(a_rev,L)
P=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
out=np.fft.irfft(A*P,L)
return out[:3*n-2][n-1:2*n-1]
```
Check output length slice n-1 to 2n-1 gives n elements. For n=1: m=1,L? conv length1; slice [0:1] okay.
We can reuse a_fft? In LSE, we need self_conv using a (not reversed) and weighted_conv using a_rev. Could compute two FFTs of a/a_rev. But self_conv needs rfft(a,Lc); weighted needs rfft(a_rev,Lb), different lengths maybe. We can cache? Not worth.

`lse_grad_z(z,beta)`:
```
def lse_grad_z(z, beta, n):
    zmax = z.max()
    ez = np.exp(z - zmax)
    a1 = ez / ez.sum()
    a = n * a1
    S = n  # by construction
    c = self_conv(a)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    score = 2.0 * n * cmax / (S*S)
    scaled = beta * (c - cmax)
    expv = np.exp(scaled)
    sumv = expv.sum()
    L = cmax + np.log(sumv) / beta
    p = expv / sumv
    b = weighted_conv(a, p)
    grad_a = 4.0 * n * b / (S*S) - 4.0 * n * L / (S*S*S)
    mean_grad = np.dot(a, grad_a) / n
    grad_z = a * (grad_a - mean_grad)
    # grad_z may have roundoff sum not zero
    return score, L, grad_z, a, c
```
S exactly n, but due a = n*a1 sum exactly n within fp. Use S = float(a.sum()) for accuracy? It should be n but maybe 1 relative. Use S for formula, and score. But z softmax enforces sum a1=1, a sum n to fp. Use S=float(a.sum()).
Numerical: `np.log(sumv) / beta`, if beta ==0? We won't use 0.
If beta very high, p one-hot, b single partner. grad_z maybe large? okay.

Could improve by using exact top K p for high beta instead of exp with overflow? Since scaled <=0, no overflow. For beta=1e5, scaled for c diff 1e-? maybe underflow; p one-hot. fine.

```
a = np.clip(a0,0,1000); s=a.sum(); if s<... uniform
a = a / s * target_n
z = np.log(a)
# center z: z -= z.mean()
```
Actually log of a; if a has zeros, log -inf. Use `z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-12))`; then center. This corresponds to floor tiny. Fine.
Then run beta phases:
```
m=np.zeros(n); v=zeros; best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score(a)
elapsed budget
for phase idx:
  beta,lr =...
  while time < deadline:
     score, _, grad_z, a, c = lse_grad_z(z,beta)
     if score < best_score: save
     # Adam step
     t +=1
     m = 0.9*m +0.1*grad_z
     v = 0.999*v +0.001*grad_z*grad_z
     mh=m/(1-0.9**t); vh=v/(1-0.999**t)
     z = z - lr * mh/(np.sqrt(vh)+1e-8)
     z = np.clip(z, -60, 60)
     # Rebuild a? Next iteration does. But for best tracking maybe check after step? We can compute next.
     # To avoid z drift, if beta phase end or every 10000, z-=mean and maybe reset? Could do:
     if t % 50000 ==0: z = z - z.mean(); m=zeros; v=zeros
  # after phase, rebuild final a and exact score
```

def mass_transfer_polish(a, time_budget, t_start, rng, max_impact_refresh=??):
    n=len(a)

    S0=float(a.sum())

    best_score=2*n*c.max()/(S*S)
    best_a=a.copy()

    # impact variables

    iterations=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       # if time since last refresh >1s or b None or accepted moves >50: recompute p,b

          b = weighted_conv(a,p)

       # recipients bottom b:

       bottom_r = np.argpartition(b, R)[:R]

       # choose j random from bottom, ensure j != i
       # For randomness, 20% j from random low half of b (not top)
       ...
       # Try fractions
       current = best_score? For accepting move, use current score (from last c) not best. We need current_score variable.
       current_score = exact from c? Reuse after each apply.
       best_frac=-1; best_new_score=current_score
       for frac in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5,0.65):
           delta=frac*a[i]
           if delta<=0: continue
           sc = test_transfer_score(a,c,S,i,j,-delta,delta)

           apply_transfer(a,c,i,j,-best_frac*a[i]) # S unchanged
           current_score=best_new_score
           if current_score < best_score: save
           accepted_streak+=1

```

Candidate score `test_transfer_score` uses c copy; for speed, compute `S2=S` since transfer. We can implement:
```
def score_after_di_dj(a,c,S,n,i,j,di,dj):
    if di==0 and dj==0: return 2*n*c.max()/(S*S)
    c2 = c.copy()
    if di != 0:
        c2[i:i+n] += (2.0*di) * a
        c2[2*i] += di*di
    if dj != 0:
        c2[j:j+n] += (2.0*dj) * a
        c2[2*j] += dj*dj
    if i != j and di != 0 and dj != 0:
        c2[i+j] += 2.0 * di * dj
    S2 = S + di + dj
    cm = float(c2.max())
    return 2.0*n*cm/(S2*S2)
```
Important: For `c2[2*i]`, if `2*i` index is within 0..2n-2 yes. For i=0, segment i:i+n includes diagonals? local index i=0, global 0; we add `2*di*a0` (which includes 2di*old_i) plus di^2 -> correct. Slice i:i+n for i=n-1 gives indices n-1..2n-2 length n, okay. For i=0 length n. Good.

def apply_di_dj(a,c,i,j,di,dj):
    if di!=0:
       c[i:i+n] += 2*di*a
       c[2*i] += di*di
    if dj!=0:
       c[j:j+n] += 2*dj*a
       c[2*j] += dj*dj

    a[i]+=di; a[j]+=dj
    # clip small negatives at i if di negative
```

def resample_sequence(a,new_n):
 n=len(a); if n==new_n return a.copy()
 x_old=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
 x_new=np.linspace(0,n-1,new_n)
 # use cubic? np.interp linear. Could use scipy interp? linear okay.
 out=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
 out=np.maximum(out,0)
 s=out.sum(); if s>0 out*=new_n/s else uniform
 return out
```

def adam_z(a0, time_budget, t_start, beta_schedule=None, lr_schedule=None, verbose=True, n_owner=None):
    n = len(a0)
    if n_owner: n = n_owner? not.
    a = np.clip(a0.astype(np.float64),0,1000.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-12: a[:] = 1.0
    else: a = a / s * n
    z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-12))
    z = z - z.mean()
    if beta_schedule is None: ...
    if lr_schedule is None: ... len match
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t_opt=0
    best_score=compute_score_from_a? use self_conv
    best_a=a.copy()

    total_phases=len(beta_schedule)
    # allocate time proportional maybe default equal? We can pass weights or use equal. Simpler equal but high beta more important. Use weights inside:
    weights = None computed based on index: more time for middle/high? Let's define function top-level `default_adam_schedule(n)`.

```
def active_prolag(c, top_frac=0.02, cap=2048):
    m=len(c); cmax=float(c.max())
    tol=0.01*cmax
    active = c >= cmax - tol
    cnt=active.sum()
    K = max(8, min(cap, int(m*top_frac)))
    if cnt > K:
       idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
       p=np.zeros(m); p[idx]=1.0/K
    else:
       p=np.zeros(m); p[active]=1.0/cnt
    # if p sum 0? p[np.argmax(c)]=1
    return p
```
If cmax=0, p uniform? return zeros.

For K~200, n=7000, O(1.4M) per refresh, maybe faster than FFT? In Python vectorized over K with loops? Could be slower. FFT okay.

def mass_transfer_polish(a, time_budget, t_start, rng):
    n=len(a); a=clip normalize to sum n; c=self_conv(a); S=n
    best_score=2*n*c.max()/S/S; best_a=a.copy()
    deadline=t_start+time_budget
    b=None; impact=None; d_top=None; r_bot=None; refresh=0; no_improve=0; recompute_count=0
    D=max(64, min(n, n//8)) # 875 for 7000
    R=max(64, min(n, n//8))
    irng = np.random.default_rng(rng)
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       if b is None or refresh>40 or no_improve>200 or time since... :
          p=active_prolag(c)
          if p.sum()==0: p uniform? 
          b=weighted_conv(a,p)
          impact=a*b
          # top donors, bottom recipients
          d_top=np.argpartition(impact, -D)[-D:]
          r_bot=np.argpartition(b, R)[:R]
          refresh=0
       # choose
       if no_improve > 300:
          # random perturbation of a? maybe small random transfer with random i,j
       # donor
       if rng.random()<0.1: i=rng.integers(n)
       else:
          cand = d_top[...]
          # filter a>0
          mask = a[cand] > 1e-10
          if mask.any(): cand=cand[mask]
          if cand.size==0: cand=d_top
          i=int(cand[int(rng.integers(cand.size))])
       # recipient
       if rng.random()<0.2:
           # choose random not too high b
           r_candidates = np.where(b < np.median(b))[0] maybe expensive each loop? Precompute median? Use bottom? We can precompute r_all = argsort? Too expensive. Use r_bot for low.
       else:
          cand=r_bot[...]
          # avoid i
          cand = cand[cand != i]
          if cand.size==0: cand = np.arange(n); cand=cand[cand!=i]
          j=int(cand[rng.integers(cand.size)])
       # evaluate fractions
       current_score = 2*n*c.max()/(S*S) (could maintain)
       best_f=0; best_sc=current_score
       ai = a[i]
       for frac in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5,0.65):
           d=frac*ai
           if d<=1e-12: break
           sc=score_after_di_dj(a,c,S,n,i,j,-d,d)
           if sc < best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_f=frac
       if best_f>0 and best_sc < current_score:
           d=best_f*a[i]
           apply_di_dj(a,c,i,j,-d,d)
           S = S # unchanged
           current_score=best_sc
           if current_score < best_score:
               best_score=current_score; best_a=a.copy()
           no_improve=0
           refresh+=1
       else:
           no_improve += 1
           refresh += 1
       if recompute_count? every 500 accepted or 2000 total: c=self_conv(a); S=a.sum(); current_score=...
    # final exact recompute
    return best_score, best_a

def generate_inits(n, rng, parent=None, count=8):
    x=np.arange(n)/float(n)
    inits=[]
    if parent is not None:
      原则 p=np.asarray(parent,float); clip; normalize; if len != n p=resample; inits append p
    inits.append(np.ones(n))
    # smooth random
    base = np.exp(0.3*rng.standard_normal(n)); smooth kernel len max(5,n//32); inits append convolve '/ sum'
    # sine modes
    for m in [1,2,3]:
       amp = 0.3+0.5*rng.random()
       ph = 2πrng.random()
       f=1+amp*np.sin(2π m x + ph)
       f=np.maximum(f,0); normalize; inits
    # two-mode antisymmetric
    for m1,m2 in [(1,2),(1,3),(2,3)]: random amp1 0.5, amp2 0.3, phases; f=1+...
    # random Fourier up to 8 with small random coefficients, clip
    # low pass random with different kernel
    # seen? limit to 10
    normalize each to sum n
    return inits
```

`lse_grad_a(a,beta)`:
```
def lse_grad_a(a,beta,n):
    S=float(a.sum())
    c=self_conv(a)
    cmax=c.max()
    score=2*n*cmax/(S*S)
    if beta<0: p=active_prolag(c); L=cmax
    else: scaled=beta*(c-cmax); expv=np.exp(scaled); sumv=expv.sum(); L=cmax+log(sumv)/beta; p=expv/sumv
    b=weighted_conv(a,p)
    grad=4*n*b/(S*S) - 4*n*L/(S*S*S)
    # optional set gradient where a=0? no
    return score,grad,a,c
```
`grad` can be negative/positive. Adam.

`adam_direct` code:
```
def adam_direct(a0, time_budget, t_start, betas=None, lrs=None, weights=None, verbose_prefix=''):
    n=len(a0)
    a=normalize_a(a0,n)
    if betas is None: betas,lrs,weights=default_adam_schedule()
    m=zeros; v=zeros; t_opt=0
    best_score=score_from_a(a,n); best_a=a.copy()
    deadline=t_start+time_budget
    phase_i=0
    for phase_i in range(len(betas)):
        phase_time = time_budget * weights[phase_i]
        phase_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + phase_time)
        beta=betas[phase_i]; lr=lrs[phase_i]
        while time.monotonic() < phase_deadline:
            score,grad,a,c = lse_grad_a(a,beta,n)
            if score < best_score: best_score=score; best_a=a.copy()
            t_opt +=1
            lr_t = lr / (1.0 + 1e-5*t_opt)
            m=0.9*m+0.1*grad
            v=0.999*v+0.001*grad*grad
            t=t_opt
            mh=m/(1-0.9**t)
            vh=v/(1-0.999**t)
            a = a - lr_t * mh/(np.sqrt(vh)+1e-8)
            a = np.clip(a,0,1000)
            s=a.sum()
            if s<=1e-8: a.fill(1.0); s=n
            else: a = a / s * n
        # end phase
    # final exact
    sc=score_from_a(a,n); if sc<best: save
    print
    return best_score,best_a
```
But time budget: If phase_time all sums equal time_budget; after first phase, time.monotonic advanced; using `phase_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic()+phase_time)` works sequentially with original weights. Good. If time_budget small, phase_time maybe 0; while skip.

```
def normalize_a(a,n):
    a = np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a = np.clip(a,0,1000)
    s=a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-12:
        a[:] = 1.0
    else:
        a = a / s * n
        # if clipping caused sum >? clipping before normalization may make values <=1000 but after scaling up could exceed1000 if s<n? We normalize to n; if some values >1000 before, after scaling may still >1000. Need clip again and iterate? Simpler: after scaling, clip to 1000 and renormalize once.
        a = np.clip(a,0,1000)
        s2=a.sum()
        if s2 <=1e-12: a[:] =1.0
        else: a = a / s2 * n
        a = np.clip(a,0,1000)
    return a
```

`rescale_with_box`:
```
def rescale_with_box(a,n):
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12: return np.ones(n)
    mx=float(a.max())
    scale = (n / s) if s > 0 else 1.0
    if mx > 0 and scale * mx > 1000.0:
        scale = 1000.0 / mx
    a = a * scale
    s2=a.sum()
    if s2 <=1e-12: return np.ones(n)
    # if still sum too low? Cannot if max binds; leave.
    return a
```
Then actual S may not n. Gradient formula uses S=float(a.sum()), target n not in score? Score uses n (length) and S. Good.

Pseudo apply:
```
def apply_two(a,c,i,j,di,dj,n):
    if di:
       c[i:i+n] += 2.0*di*a
       c[2*i] += di*di
    if dj:
       c[j:j+n] += 2.0*dj*a
       c[2*j] += dj*dj
    if i != j and di and dj:
       c[i+j] += 2.0*di*dj
    a[i] += di
    a[j] += dj
```

Now, screen vs main time. We need implement all top-level. Let's sketch `propose_candidate`:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    B=max(30.0, float(budget_s)) - 20.0
    deadline=t0+B
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    parent=None
    try:
       p=getip? globals().get('height_sequence_1')
       if p is not None:
          arr=np.asarray(p,dtype=float).ravel()
          if arr.size>=32:
             parent=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
    except: parent=None
    # choose n_main
    if parent is not None:
        np_=len(parent)
        if np_ >= 2048:
            n_main = np_ if np_ <= 16384 else 8192
        else:
            n_main = 4096
    else:
        n_main = 4096
    # Maybe if n_main too small, 2048? Use 4096.
    inits=generate_inits(n_main, rng, parent, count=6 if parent else 8)
    # ensure parent init first and normalized
    # screen
    screen_B = max(20, B*0.10)
    n_in=len(inits)
    per_screen=screen_B/n_in
    screen_results=[]
    short_betas=[1,10,-1]; short_lrs=[0.08,0.05,0.03]; short_weights=[0.4,0.4,0.2]
    for idx,a in enumerate(inits):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 120: break
       tcur=time.monotonic()
       sc,a2=adam_direct(a, max(2, per_screen*0.75), tcur, short_betas, short_lrs, short_weights)
       a2=normalize_sequence(a2,n_main)
       if per_screen*0.25 > 3:
           tcur2=time.monotonic()
           sc2,a2=mass_transfer_polish(a2, min(per_screen*0.25, deadline-tcur2-120), tcur2, rng)
       else:
           sc2=sc; a2=a2
       screen_results.append((sc2,a2))
       print(...)
    sort, best_sc,best_a=...
    # Main adam on best
    tcur=time.monotonic(); main_budget=max(0, min(B*0.35, deadline-tcur-200))
    if main_budget>30:
        sc,a=adam_direct(best_a, main_budget, tcur, default)
        if sc<best_sc: best...
    # Main adam on second if exists and time
    if len(screen)>1:
       tcur=time.monotonic(); second_budget=max(0,min(B*0.10, deadline-tcur-200))
       if second_budget>30:
          sc2,a2=adam_direct(screen[1][1], second_budget, tcur, default)
          if sc2<best_sc: best=a2
    # Polish best
    tcur=time.monotonic(); pol_budget=max(0,min(B*0.35, deadline-tcur-60))
    if pol_budget>20:
       sc,a=mass_transfer_polish(best_a, pol_budget, tcur, rng)
       if sc<best_sc: best...
    # Extra loop random restarts/polish
    tcur=time.monotonic()
    if deadline - tcur > 120:
       # perturb best
       b=best_a.copy()
       noise=0.05*rng.standard_normal(n_main)
       b = b*(1+noise); b=np.maximum(b,0)
       b=normalize_sequence(b,n_main)
       tcur2=time.monotonic(); re_budget=min(120, (deadline-tcur2)*0.4)
       if re_budget>20:
          sc,a=adam_direct(b, re_budget, tcur2, short_betas, short_lrs, short_weights)
          if sc<best_sc: best=a
       tcur3=time.monotonic(); p_budget=max(0, deadline-tcur3-20)
       if p_budget>20:
          sc,a=mass_transfer_polish(best_a, p_budget, tcur3, rng)
          if sc<best_sc: best=a
    # final rescale/verify
```

```
def build_a_from_x(x,n):
    xc=x - x.max()
    e=np.exp(xc)
    s=e.sum()
    scale = n / s
    if scale * 1.0 > 1000: scale = 1000.0
    a=e*scale
    s2=a.sum()
    if s2 <=1e-12: a=np.ones(n)
    return a
```

```
def x_from_a(a,n):
    a = np.clip(a,0,1000)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=...: a=np.ones(n)
    else: a = a / s * n
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-8)
    x = np.log(a / n)  # mean log ~0 if sum n
    x = x - np.mean(x)
    x = np.clip(x, -10,10)
    return x
```

`active_prolag(c)`:
```
def active_prolag(c):
    m=len(c); cmax=float(c.max())
    if cmax <= 1e-12:
       return np.ones(m)/m
    cmin=float(c.min())
    if cmax - cmin < 1e-8*max(1.0,cmax):
       return np.ones(m)/m
    tol=0.01*cmax
    active = c >= cmax - tol
    cnt=int(active.sum())
    K=max(16, min(m, 32 + m//64))
    if cnt > K:
       idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
       p=np.zeros(m); p[idx]=1.0/K
    else:
       p=np.zeros(m); p[active]=1.0/cnt
    return p
```
If cnt==0? active at cmax at least 1. Good.

`lse_grad_a(a,beta,n)`:
```
def lse_grad_a(a,beta,n):
    S=float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 1e-12: return inf, zeros,...
    c=self_conv(a)
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    score=2.0*n*cmax/(S*S)
    if beta is None or beta < 0:
        p=active_prolag(c); L=cmax
    else:
        scaled=beta*(c-cmax)
        # if beta huge, scaled safe (<=0)
        expv=np.exp(scaled)
        sv=float(np.sum(expv))
        if sv <=0 or not finite: p=active_prolag(c); L=cmax
        else:
            p=expv/sv
            L=cmax + np.log(sv)/beta
    b=weighted_conv(a,p)
    grad = (4.0*n/S**2)*b - (4.0*n*L/S**3)
    # replace nonfinite
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)): grad=np.nan_to_num(grad)
    return score, grad, a, c
```
`S**3` maybe; use S2.

`build_a_from_x(x,n)`:
```
def build_a_from_x(x,n):
    xmax=np.max(x)
    if not finite: return np.ones(n)
    xc=x-xmax
    e=np.exp(np.clip(xc,-50,0))
    s=float(np.sum(e))
    if s<=1e-12: return np.ones(n)
    scale = n / s
    if scale > 1000.0: scale=1000.0
    if scale <=0: scale=1.0
    a=e*scale
    # If due to scale cap sum may be far; okay
    return a
```

`normalize_sequence`:
```
def normalize_sequence(a, n_target):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if a.size != n_target: # if wrong, resample? Not here.
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s <=1e-12:
        return np.ones(float(n_target))
    mx=float(a.max())
    scale= n_target / s
    if mx > 0 and scale * mx > 1000.0:
        scale = 1000.0 / mx
    a = a * scale
    # After clamp, if sum <=0 fallback
    s2=float(a.sum())
    if s2 <=1e-12:
       return np.ones(float(n_target))
    # if max still >1000 due rounding
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    return a
```
If scale capped and sum maybe < target, okay.
Uniform ones sum n_target, max1, scale1.

def x_from_a(a,n):
    a=normalize_sequence(a,n)
    a=np.maximum(a,1e-10)
    x=np.log(a / n)
    x = x - np.mean(x)
    x = np.clip(x, -12, 12)
    return x

def adam_smooth(...):
    n=len(a0)
    a=normalize_sequence(a0,n)
    x=x_from_a(a,n)
    if betas is None: betas,lrs,weights=default_adam_schedule()
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t_opt=0
    best_score=compute_score(a,n)? use self_conv
    best_a=a.copy()
    deadline=t_start+time_budget
    for idx in range(len(betas)):
       phase_time = time_budget * weights[idx]
       phase_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic()+phase_time)
       beta=betas[idx]; lr=lrs[idx]
       while time.monotonic() < phase_deadline:
           a=build_a_from_x(x,n)
           score, grad_a, a, c = lse_grad_a(a,beta,n)
           if score < best_score and finite: best...
           grad_x = a * grad_a
           # if nonfinite break?
           if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_x)):
               grad_x = np.nan_to_num(grad_x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
           t_opt +=1
           lr_t = lr / (1.0 + 1e-5*t_opt)
           m = 0.9*m + 0.1*grad_x
           v = 0.999*v + 0.001*grad_x*grad_x
           mh = m/(1-0.9**t_opt)
           vh = v/(1-0.999**t_opt)
           x = x - lr_t * mh/(np.sqrt(vh)+1e-8)
           x = np.clip(x, -12, 12)
           if t_opt % 10000 ==0:
               x = x - np.mean(x)
    # final
    a=build_a_from_x(x,n)
    sc=compute_score(a,n)
    if sc<best: save
    print
    return best_score,best_a
```

def mass_transfer_polish(a, time_budget, t_start, rng):
    n=len(a)
    a=normalize_sequence(a,n)
    S=float(a.sum())
    c=self_conv(a)
    best_score=2*n*c.max()/(S*S)
    best_a=a.copy()
    deadline=t_start+time_budget
    if time_budget <=0: return best_score,best_a
    b=None; impact=None; top_d=None; bot_r=None
    refresh=0; no_improve=0; move_count=0
    current_score=best_score
    D=max(32, min(n, n//8))
    R=max(32, min(n, n//8))
    median_b=None
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       if b is None or refresh > 60 or no_improve > 300 or move_count % 2000 ==0:
           p=active_prolag(c)
           b=weighted_conv(a,p)
           impact=a*b
           if D >= n:
              top_d=np.arange(n)
           else: top_d=np.argpartition(impact, -D)[-D:]
           if R >= n:
              bot_r=np.arange(n)
           else: bot_r=np.argpartition(b, R)[:R]
           median_b=float(np.median(b))
           refresh=0
       if no_improve > 2000:
           # shake

           a[idx] *= rng.uniform(0.5,2.0,size=idx.size)
           a=np.maximum(a,0)

           S=float(a.sum()); c=self_conv(a); current_score=2*n*c.max()/(S*S)
           b=None; no_improve=0; move_count=0
           if current_score < best_score: best...
       # choose i
       if rng.random() < 0.1:
           i=int(rng.integers(n))
       else:
           # filter top_d for a>1e-12 maybe
           cand=top_d
           mask = a[cand] > 1e-10
           if mask.any(): cand=cand[mask]
           if cand.size == 0: cand=top_d

       else:
           cand=bot_r
           cand2=cand[cand != i]
           if cand2.size == 0:
               j=int(rng.integers(n))
               if j==i: j=(j+1)%n

               j=int(cand2[int(rng.integers(cand2.size))])
       ai=a[i]
       if ai <= 1e-12:
           no_improve +=1; refresh+=1; continue
       # If b[j] > b[i] maybe skip? optional, but allow if rng? Use if b[j] < b[i]*1.2 or random.

           no_improve +=1; refresh+=1; continue
       old_current=current_score
       best_frac=0.0; best_new=current_score
       # fractions maybe fewer if time
       for frac in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5):
           d=frac*ai
           if d<=0: break
           sc=score_after_two(a,c,S,n,i,j,-d,d)
           if sc < best_new:
               best_new=sc; best_frac=frac
       if best_frac>0 and best_new < current_score:
           d=best_frac*a[i]
           apply_two_changes(a,c,i,j,-d,d,n)
           a[i]=max(a[i],0.0); a[j]=max(a[j],0.0)
           S=S # unchanged
           current_score=best_new
           move_count +=1
           no_improve=0
           refresh +=1
           if current_score < best_score:
               best_score=current_score; best_a=a.copy()
       else:
           no_improve +=1
           refresh +=1

    c=self_conv(a); S=a.sum(); sc=2*n*c.max()/(S*S)

`apply_two_changes`:
```
def apply_two_changes(a,c,i,j,di,dj,n):
    if di !=0:
       c[i:i+n] += (2.0*di)*a
       c[2*i] += di*di
    if dj !=0:
       c[j:j+n] += (2.0*dj)*a
       c[2*j] += dj*dj
    if i != j and di !=0 and dj !=0:
       c[i+j] += 2.0*di*dj
    a[i] += di
    a[j] += dj
```
But if `2*i` or `2*j` outside? no.
If we call with di=-d,dj=+d, and `a[i]` maybe updated after; we must not have changed a before. Good.

`score_after_two`:
```
def score_after_two(a,c,S,n,i,j,di,dj):
    c2 = c.copy()
    if di !=0:
       c2[i:i+n] += (2.0*di)*a
       c2[2*i] += di*di
    if dj !=0:
       c2[j:j+n] += (2.0*dj)*a
       c2[2*j] += dj*dj
    if i != j and di !=0 and dj !=0:
       c2[i+j] += 2.0*di*dj
    S2 = S + di + dj
    if S2 <= 1e-12: return np.inf
    cm = float(np.max(c2))
    return 2.0*n*cm/(S2*S2)
```
For transfer S2=S.

Potential issue: `c2[i:i+n] += (2.0*di)*a` creates full a length n but only slice length n; for i near n-1, `a` full length n, slice length n. Good. It uses all a values, but for k segment i..i+n-1, partner j index range 0..n-1. Yes. If a length n, okay. Wastes? no.
But for i>0, the segment c2[i:i+n] length n, adding `2*di*a[0:n]`. Correct mapping k=i+j, partner value a[j]. Good.

Now, if `c` has length 2n-1, `c2[i;i+n]` for i=n-1 length n indices n-1..2n-2; okay.

Now, `resample_sequence` top-level:
```
def resample_sequence(a,new_n):
    n=len(a)
    if new_n <=0: return np.array([1.0])
    if n == new_n: return a.copy()
    if n ==1: return np.full(new_n, a[0])
    x_old=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,n-1.0,new_n)
    out=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
    out=np.maximum(out,0.0)
    return normalize_sequence(out,new_n)
```
`normalize_sequence` expects length new_n; okay.

```
def generate_inits(n,rng,parent=None,count=6):
    inits=[]
    def add? no nested. Use loops.
    if parent is not None:
       p=np.asarray(parent,float).ravel(); p=np.clip(p,0,1000); if p.size>0: inits.append(normalize_sequence(resample_sequence(p,n), n))
    inits.append(np.ones(n))
    # smooth random 2 variants
    for kfactor in [32,16]:
       base=np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n)) + 0.5
       k=max(3, n//kfactor)
       kern=np.ones(k)/k
       sm=np.convolve(base,kern,mode='same')
       inits.append(normalize_sequence(sm,n))
    # sine modes
    x=np.arange(n)/float(n)
    for m in [1,2,3]:
       amp=0.4+0.4*rng.random()
       ph=2*np.pi*rng.random()
       f=1.0 + amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*x + ph)
       inits.append(normalize_sequence(np.maximum(f,1e-6),n))
    # two modes
    for m1,m2 in [(1,2),(1,3)]:
       amp1=0.5+0.4*rng.random(); amp2=0.2+0.3*rng.random()
       ph1=2πrng.random(); ph2=...
       f=1 + amp1*sin(2πm1 x+ph1)+amp2*sin(2πm2 x+ph2)
       inits.append(normalize_sequence(np.maximum(f,1e-6),n))
    # random Fourier
    coeffs=[0.0 for _ in range(min(8,n))]
    for m in range(1,min(8,n)+1):
        coeff = (rng.random()-0.5)*0.5
        f += coeff * np.sin(2π m x)
    # maybe use existing f? Simpler create one:
    f=1.0
    for m in range(1,8): f += (rng.random()-0.5)*0.4 * np.sin(2π m x)
    inits.append(normalize_sequence(...))
    # if parent not None, add perturbed parent
    if inits[0] parent:
       pert = inits[0] * (1 + 0.05*rng.standard_normal(n)); inits.append(normalize_sequence(np.maximum(pert,0),n))
    # limit count
    if count and len(inits)>count: inits=inits[:count]
    return inits
```

```
def sequence_score(a,n):
    S=float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 1e-12: return np.inf
    c=self_conv(a)
    return float(2.0*n*np.max(c)/(S*S))
```

Now, `propose_candidate` details. Let's code robust:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = max(30.0, float(budget_s))
    B = max(30.0, total_budget - 20.0)
    deadline = t0 + B
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    parent = None
    try:
       p = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
       if p is not None:
          arr = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
          if arr.size >= 32:
             parent = np.clip(arr,0,1000)
    except Exception:
       parent=None
    if parent is not None and len(parent) < 512:
       n_main = 4096
    elif parent is not None:
       n_main = len(parent)
       if n_main < 2048: n_main=4096
       if n_main > 20000: n_main=16384
    else:
       n_main=4096
    # Maybe cap to 16384 to avoid too slow
    if n_main > 16384: n_main=16384
```
If parent length 6962, n_main 6962. If no parent 4096. If parent too long 20000 cap 16384.

```python
"""
Hybrid optimizer for non-negative sequences minimizing:

    2 * n * max(self-convolution) / (sum(sequence))^2

Strategy:
- Work in log-space with a smooth LSE approximation to the max convolution.
- Use Adam in log-space, which keeps sequences strictly positive and respects
  the evaluator's [0, 1000] box.
- Use exact top-lag active-set subgradients for late-stage focus.
- Apply a fast exact mass-transfer/local-search polish using O(n) convolution
  updates. This moves probability from high-peak-contributing coordinates to
  low-impact coordinates and often escapes shallow local minima.
- Multi-stage search: short screening, longer refinement, exact polish, and a
  final optional upsampling/refinement pass.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def self_conv(a):
    """Linear self-convolution using FFT."""
    n = len(a)
    need = 2 * n - 1
    L = next_pow2(need)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)[:need]
    if c.size and c.min() < 0.0:
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def weighted_conv(a, p):
    """
    Return b_i = sum_j p_{i+j} a_j for i=0..n-1, where len(a)=n and len(p)=2n-1.
    This is the gradient building block for weighted convolutions.
    """
    n = len(a)
    need = 3 * n - 2
    L = next_pow2(need)
    ar = a[::-1]
    A = np.fft.rfft(ar, L)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * P, L)[:need]
    return out[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def active_prolag(c):
    """
    Return a probability vector over convolution lags.
    Uses all lags near the maximum, or the top K lags if there are too many.
    """
    m = len(c)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if cmax <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return np.ones(m) / m

    cmin = float(np.min(c))
    if cmax - cmin < 1e-8 * max(1.0, cmax):
        return np.ones(m) / m

    tol = 0.02 * cmax
    active = c >= (cmax - tol)
    cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(active))

    K = max(64, 128 + m // 32)
    K = min(K, m)

    if cnt > K:
        if K >= m:
            idx = np.arange(m)
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
        p = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        p[idx] = 1.0 / K
    else:
        p = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        p[active] = 1.0 / max(1, cnt)

    if not np.any(p > 0):
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    return p


def normalize_sequence(a, n_target):
    """Normalize to sum n_target while keeping values in [0, 1000]."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)

    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return np.ones(n_target, dtype=np.float64)

    mx = float(np.max(a))
    scale = float(n_target) / s
    if mx > 0.0 and scale * mx > 1000.0:
        scale = 1000.0 / mx

    a = a * scale
    s2 = float(np.sum(a))
    if s2 <= 1e-12:
        return np.ones(n_target, dtype=np.float64)
    return np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)


def resample_sequence(a, new_n):
    """Resample a non-negative sequence to new length by linear interpolation."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = len(a)
    if new_n <= 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == new_n:
        out = a.copy()
    elif n == 1:
        out = np.full(new_n, float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        x_old = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
        x_new = np.linspace(0.0, float(n - 1), new_n, dtype=np.float64)
        out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)

    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    return normalize_sequence(out, new_n)


def sequence_score(a, n):
    """Exact score: 2*n*max(self-conv)/(sum)^2."""
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    c = self_conv(a)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * float(n) * cmax / (S * S))


def lse_grad_a(a, beta, n):
    """
    LSE-smoothed score and gradient with respect to a.
    If beta < 0, use exact top-lag active-set subgradient.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 1e-12:
        grad = np.zeros_like(a, dtype=np.float64)
        c = self_conv(a)
        return float("inf"), grad, a, c

    c = self_conv(a)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        grad = np.zeros_like(a, dtype=np.float64)
        return float("inf"), grad, a, c

    score = float(2.0 * float(n) * cmax / (S * S))

    if beta is None or beta < 0:
        p = active_prolag(c)
        L = cmax
    else:
        scaled = beta * (c - cmax)
        expv = np.exp(scaled)
        sv = float(np.sum(expv))
        if sv <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(sv):
            p = active_prolag(c)
            L = cmax
        else:
            p = expv / sv
            L = cmax + float(np.log(sv)) / float(beta)

    b = weighted_conv(a, p)

    S2 = S * S
    grad = (4.0 * float(n) / S2) * b - (4.0 * float(n) * L / (S2 * S))

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

    return score, grad, a, c


def build_a_from_x(x, n):
    """
    Convert log variables x to sequence a = n * softmax(x), with box constraint.
    If the natural scale would exceed 1000, scale down instead.
    """
    if len(x) != n:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    xmax = float(np.max(x))
    if not np.isfinite(xmax):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    xc = x - xmax
    xc = np.clip(xc, -50.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(xc)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    scale = float(n) / s
    if scale > 1000.0:
        scale = 1000.0
    if scale <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(scale):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    a = e * scale
    if float(np.sum(a)) <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)


def x_from_a(a, n):
    """Initialize log variables from a sequence."""
    a = normalize_sequence(a, n)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    x = np.log(a / float(n))
    x = x - float(np.mean(x))
    return np.clip(x, -12.0, 12.0)


def default_adam_schedule():
    betas = [1.0, 4.0, 12.0, 40.0, 120.0, -1.0]
    lrs = [0.15, 0.12, 0.10, 0.08, 0.06, 0.05]
    weights = [0.15, 0.20, 0.20, 0.20, 0.15, 0.10]
    return betas, lrs, weights


def short_adam_schedule():
    betas = [1.0, 10.0, -1.0]
    lrs = [0.20, 0.12, 0.08]
    weights = [0.40, 0.40, 0.20]
    return betas, lrs, weights


def medium_adam_schedule():
    betas = [1.0, 4.0, 12.0, 40.0, -1.0]
    lrs = [0.16, 0.13, 0.10, 0.08, 0.06]
    weights = [0.20, 0.25, 0.25, 0.20, 0.10]
    return betas, lrs, weights


def adam_smooth(a0, time_budget, t_start, betas=None, lrs=None, weights=None, name=""):
    """
    Log-space Adam with LSE continuation and exact active-set subgradients.
    """
    time_budget = max(0.0, float(time_budget))
    n = len(a0)

    a = normalize_sequence(a0, n)
    x = x_from_a(a, n)

    if betas is None:
        betas, lrs, weights = default_adam_schedule()

    while len(lrs) < len(betas):
        lrs.append(float(lrs[-1]))
    while len(weights) < len(betas):
        weights.append(float(weights[-1]))

    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t_opt = 0

    best_score = sequence_score(a, n)
    best_a = a.copy()

    deadline = t_start + time_budget

    for ph in range(len(betas)):
        phase_time = time_budget * float(weights[ph])
        phase_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + phase_time)
        beta = float(betas[ph])
        lr = float(lrs[ph])

        while time.monotonic() < phase_deadline:
            a = build_a_from_x(x, n)
            score, grad_a, a, _c = lse_grad_a(a, beta, n)

            if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
                best_score = float(score)
                best_a = a.copy()

            if not np.isfinite(score):
                break

            S = float(np.sum(a))
            if S <= 1e-12:
                break

            # Log-space gradient. The weighted mean subtraction removes constant
            # log drift and is exact up to homogeneity.
            mean_grad = float(np.dot(a, grad_a)) / S
            grad_x = a * (grad_a - mean_grad)

            if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_x)):
                grad_x = np.nan_to_num(grad_x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

            t_opt += 1
            lr_t = lr / (1.0 + 1e-5 * t_opt)

            m = 0.9 * m + 0.1 * grad_x
            v = 0.999 * v + 0.001 * grad_x * grad_x

            bias1 = 1.0 - 0.9 ** t_opt
            bias2 = 1.0 - 0.999 ** t_opt
            if bias1 <= 1e-15:
                mh = m
            else:
                mh = m / bias1
            if bias2 <= 1e-15:
                vh = v
            else:
                vh = v / bias2

            step = mh / (np.sqrt(vh) + 1e-8)
            x = x - lr_t * step
            x = np.clip(x, -12.0, 12.0)

            if t_opt % 10000 == 0:
                x = x - float(np.mean(x))

    a = build_a_from_x(x, n)
    sc = sequence_score(a, n)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
        best_score = float(sc)
        best_a = a.copy()

    label = name if name else "adam"
    print(f"[{label}] score={best_score:.8f} n={n} time={time.monotonic()-t_start:.1f}s")
    return best_score, best_a


def score_after_two(a, c, S, n, i, j, di, dj):
    """
    Exact score after changing a[i] by di and a[j] by dj.
    Uses O(n) convolution update, no FFT.
    """
    if di == 0.0 and dj == 0.0:
        if S <= 1e-12:
            return float("inf")
        cm = float(np.max(c))
        return float(2.0 * float(n) * cm / (S * S))

    c2 = c.copy()

    if di != 0.0:
        c2[i:i + n] += (2.0 * di) * a
        c2[2 * i] += di * di

    if dj != 0.0:
        c2[j:j + n] += (2.0 * dj) * a
        c2[2 * j] += dj * dj

    if i != j and di != 0.0 and dj != 0.0:
        c2[i + j] += 2.0 * di * dj

    S2 = S + di + dj
    if S2 <= 1e-12:
        return float("inf")

    cm = float(np.max(c2))
    if not np.isfinite(cm):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * float(n) * cm / (S2 * S2))


def apply_two_changes(a, c, i, j, di, dj, n):
    """Apply exact changes di to a[i] and dj to a[j], updating c in place."""
    if di != 0.0:
        c[i:i + n] += (2.0 * di) * a
        c[2 * i] += di * di

    if dj != 0.0:
        c[j:j + n] += (2.0 * dj) * a
        c[2 * j] += dj * dj

    if i != j and di != 0.0 and dj != 0.0:
        c[i + j] += 2.0 * di * dj

    a[i] += di
    a[j] += dj

    if a[i] < 0.0:
        a[i] = 0.0
    if a[j] < 0.0:
        a[j] = 0.0
    if a[i] > 1000.0:
        a[i] = 1000.0
    if a[j] > 1000.0:
        a[j] = 1000.0


def mass_transfer_polish(a, time_budget, t_start, rng):
    """
    Exact greedy mass-transfer polish.

    Maintains the full convolution c and applies O(n) updates. It moves mass
    from high-convolution-impact coordinates to low-impact coordinates.
    """
    time_budget = max(0.0, float(time_budget))
    n = len(a)

    a = normalize_sequence(a, n)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    c = self_conv(a)

    if S <= 1e-12:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        S = float(n)
        c = self_conv(a)

    current_score = sequence_score(a, n)
    best_score = float(current_score)
    best_a = a.copy()

    deadline = t_start + time_budget
    if time_budget <= 0.0:
        return best_score, best_a

    D = max(32, n // 8)
    D = min(D, n)
    R = max(32, n // 8)
    R = min(R, n)

    b = None
    impact = None
    top_d = None
    bot_r = None

    refresh = 0
    no_improve = 0
    move_count = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if b is None or refresh > 60 or no_improve > 300 or move_count % 2000 == 0:
            p = active_prolag(c)
            b = weighted_conv(a, p)
            impact = a * b

            if D >= n:
                top_d = np.arange(n)
            else:
                top_d = np.argpartition(impact, -D)[-D:]

            if R >= n:
                bot_r = np.arange(n)
            else:
                bot_r = np.argpartition(b, R)[:R]

            refresh = 0

        if no_improve > 2000:
            # Shake: random multiplicative perturbation to escape local minima.
            n_shake = min(n, 2000)
            idx = rng.integers(0, n, size=n_shake)
            factors = rng.uniform(0.5, 2.0, size=n_shake)
            a[idx] *= factors
            a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
            a = normalize_sequence(a, n)
            S = float(np.sum(a))
            c = self_conv(a)
            current_score = sequence_score(a, n)
            b = None
            impact = None
            no_improve = 0
            move_count = 0
            if current_score < best_score:
                best_score = float(current_score)
                best_a = a.copy()

        # Choose donor.
        if float(rng.random()) < 0.10:
            i = int(rng.integers(0, n))
        else:
            cand = top_d
            good = a[cand] > 1e-10
            if np.any(good):
                cand = cand[good]
            if cand.size == 0:
                cand = top_d
            i = int(cand[int(rng.integers(0, cand.size))])

        # Choose recipient.
        if float(rng.random()) < 0.20:
            j = int(rng.integers(0, n))
            if j == i:
                j = (j + 1) % n
        else:
            cand = bot_r
            cand = cand[cand != i]
            if cand.size == 0:
                j = int(rng.integers(0, n))
                if j == i:
                    j = (j + 1) % n
            else:
                j = int(cand[int(rng.integers(0, cand.size))])

        ai = float(a[i])
        if ai <= 1e-12:
            no_improve += 1
            refresh += 1
            continue

        # Usually avoid obviously bad transfers according to current impact.
        if b[j] > b[i] + 1e-12 and float(rng.random()) < 0.70:
            no_improve += 1
            refresh += 1
            continue

        best_frac = 0.0
        best_new = float(current_score)

        for frac in (0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35, 0.50):
            d = float(frac * ai)
            room = 1000.0 - float(a[j])
            if room < d:
                d = room
            if d <= 1e-12:
                break

            sc = score_after_two(a, c, S, n, i, j, -d, d)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_new:
                best_new = float(sc)
                best_frac = float(frac)

        if best_frac > 0.0 and best_new < current_score:
            d = float(best_frac * a[i])
            apply_two_changes(a, c, i, j, -d, d, n)
            # S unchanged for transfers within [0, 1000].
            current_score = best_new
            move_count += 1
            no_improve = 0
            refresh += 1

            if current_score < best_score:
                best_score = float(current_score)
                best_a = a.copy()
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            refresh += 1

        # Periodically reconcile against FFT to kill accumulated drift.
        if move_count > 0 and move_count % 1000 == 0:
            S = float(np.sum(a))
            c = self_conv(a)
            current_score = sequence_score(a, n)
            b = None
            impact = None
            refresh = 0
            if current_score < best_score:
                best_score = float(current_score)
                best_a = a.copy()

    S = float(np.sum(a))
    c = self_conv(a)
    final_score = sequence_score(a, n)
    if final_score < best_score:
        best_score = float(final_score)
        best_a = a.copy()

    print(f"[mass-transfer] score={best_score:.8f} n={n} time={time.monotonic()-t_start:.1f}s")
    return best_score, best_a


def generate_inits(n, rng, parent=None, count=8):
    """Generate diverse starting sequences of length n."""
    inits = []
    two_pi = 2.0 * np.pi
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, n))

    if parent is not None:
        p = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if p.size > 0:
            inits.append(normalize_sequence(resample_sequence(p, n), n))

    # Uniform baseline.
    inits.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    # Smoothed random positive fields.
    for kfactor in (32, 16):
        base = np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n)) + 0.5
        k = max(3, n // kfactor)
        kern = np.ones(k, dtype=np.float64) / float(k)
        sm = np.convolve(base, kern, mode="same")
        inits.append(normalize_sequence(np.maximum(sm, 1e-6), n))

    # Single sinusoidal modulations.
    for mm in (1, 2, 3):
        amp = 0.4 + 0.4 * float(rng.random())
        ph = two_pi * float(rng.random())
        f = 1.0 + amp * np.sin(two_pi * mm * x + ph)
        inits.append(normalize_sequence(np.maximum(f, 1e-6), n))

    # Two-mode sinusoidal modulations.
    for mm1, mm2 in ((1, 2), (1, 3)):
        amp1 = 0.5 + 0.4 * float(rng.random())
        amp2 = 0.2 + 0.3 * float(rng.random())
        ph1 = two_pi * float(rng.random())
        ph2 = two_pi * float(rng.random())
        f = 1.0 + amp1 * np.sin(two_pi * mm1 * x + ph1) + amp2 * np.sin(two_pi * mm2 * x + ph2)
        inits.append(normalize_sequence(np.maximum(f, 1e-6), n))

    # Random low-order Fourier modulation.
    f = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for mm in range(1, min(8, n) + 1):
        coef = (float(rng.random()) - 0.5) * 0.45
        f = f + coef * np.sin(two_pi * mm * x)
    inits.append(normalize_sequence(np.maximum(f, 1e-6), n))

    # If we started from parent, add a perturbed copy.
    if parent is not None and len(inits) >= 2:
        p0 = inits[0]
        pert = p0 * (1.0 + 0.05 * rng.standard_normal(n))
        inits.append(normalize_sequence(np.maximum(pert, 1e-6), n))

    # Ensure all are good.
    cleaned = []
    for a in inits:
        if a is None:
            continue
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        if a.size != n:
            a = resample_sequence(a, n)
        a = normalize_sequence(a, n)
        if np.isfinite(a).all() and np.sum(a) > 1e-8:
            cleaned.append(a)

    if not cleaned:
        cleaned.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    if count is not None and len(cleaned) > int(count):
        cleaned = cleaned[:int(count)]

    return cleaned


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    """
    Main entry point.
    Searches for a non-negative sequence minimizing the evaluation score.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = max(30.0, float(budget_s))
    B = max(30.0, total_budget - 20.0)
    deadline = t0 + B
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Load parent construction if present.
    parent = None
    try:
        p = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if p is not None:
            arr = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if arr.size >= 32:
                parent = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    # Choose main working length.
    if parent is not None:
        nparent = len(parent)
        if nparent < 512:
            n_main = 4096
        elif nparent < 2048:
            n_main = 4096
        elif nparent > 20000:
            n_main = 16384
        else:
            n_main = nparent
    else:
        n_main = 4096

    n_main = int(max(512, int(n_main)))
    if n_main > 20000:
        n_main = 16384

    print(f"[setup] n_main={n_main}, parent={'yes' if parent is not None else 'no'}")

    init_count = 6 if parent is not None else 8
    inits = generate_inits(n_main, rng, parent, init_count)
    if not inits:
        inits = [np.ones(n_main, dtype=np.float64)]

    best_score = float("inf")
    best_a = None

    # -------------------------
    # 1) Short screening
    # -------------------------
    if parent is not None:
        screen_budget = max(60.0, B * 0.08)
    else:
        screen_budget = max(120.0, B * 0.12)
    screen_budget = min(screen_budget, B * 0.25)

    short_betas, short_lrs, short_weights = short_adam_schedule()
    per = screen_budget / float(max(1, len(inits)))

    screen_results = []
    for idx, a0 in enumerate(inits):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now > deadline - 180:
            break

        tb = 0.75 * per
        tb = min(tb, max(0.0, deadline - now - 220))
        if tb < 3.0:
            tb = max(0.0, deadline - now - 220)

        sc, a1 = adam_smooth(a0, tb, now, short_betas, short_lrs, short_weights, f"screen{idx}")
        a1 = normalize_sequence(a1, n_main)

        tb2 = 0.30 * per
        tb2 = min(tb2, max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 180))
        if tb2 > 4.0:
            sc, a1 = mass_transfer_polish(a1, tb2, time.monotonic(), rng)

        if ready:
            pass
        screen_results.append((sc, a1))
        print(f"[screen {idx}] best={sc:.8f}")

        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_a = np.clip(a1, 0.0, 1000.0).copy()

    if not screen_results:
        a_plain = normalize_sequence(np.ones(n_main), n_main)
        sc = sequence_score(a_plain, n_main)
        screen_results = [(sc, a_plain)]
        best_score = sc
        best_a = a_plain.copy()

    screen_results.sort()
    if screen_results[0][0] < best_score:
        best_score = float(screen_results[0][0])
        best_a = np.clip(screen_results[0][1], 0.0, 1000.0).copy()

    print(f"[after screening] best={best_score:.8f}")

    # -------------------------
    # 2) Main refinement
    # -------------------------
    now = time.monotonic()
    main_budget = min(B * 0.32, max(0.0, deadline - now - 350))
    if main_budget > 60.0:
        betas, lrs, weights = default_adam_schedule()
        sc, a1 = adam_smooth(best_a, main_budget, now, betas, lrs, weights, "main")
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_a = np.clip(a1, 0.0, 1000.0).copy()

    # Optional refinement of second-best screen result.
    if len(screen_results) > 1:
        now = time.monotonic()
        second_budget = min(B * 0.08, max(0.0, deadline - now - 320))
        if second_budget > 60.0:
            second_init = screen_results[1][1]
            # If second is not dramatically worse, it may lead to a different basin.
            if float(screen_results[1][0]) <= best_score * 1.02 + 1e-6:
                betas, lrs, weights = medium_adam_schedule()
                sc, a1 = adam_smooth(second_init, second_budget, now, betas, lrs, weights, "second")
                if sc < best_score:
                    best_score = float(sc)
                    best_a = np.clip(a1, 0.0, 1000.0).copy()

    # -------------------------
    # 3) Exact mass-transfer polish
    # -------------------------
    now = time.monotonic()
    polish_budget = min(B * 0.28, max(0.0, deadline - now - 250))
    if polish_budget > 30.0:
        sc, a1 = mass_transfer_polish(best_a, polish_budget, now, rng)
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_a = np.clip(a1, 0.0, 1000.0).copy()

    # -------------------------
    # 4) Optional upsampling / final polish
    # -------------------------
    now = time.monotonic()
    remaining = max(0.0, deadline - now - 30)

    if remaining > 300.0 and len(best_a) < 8192:
        n_up = 8192
        a_up = resample_sequence(best_a, n_up)

        tb = min(600.0, remaining * 0.55)
        if tb > 60.0:
            betas, lrs, weights = medium_adam_schedule()
            sc, a1 = adam_smooth(a_up, tb, now, betas, lrs, weights, "upsample")
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc)
                best_a = np.clip(a1, 0.0, 1000.0).copy()

        now = time.monotonic()
        tb2 = max(0.0, deadline - now - 20)
        if tb2 > 20.0:
            sc, a1 = mass_transfer_polish(best_a, tb2, now, rng)
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc)
                best_a = np.clip(a1, 0.0, 1000.0).copy()
    else:
        now = time.monotonic()
        tb = max(0.0, deadline - now - 20)
        if tb > 20.0:
            sc, a1 = mass_transfer_polish(best_a, tb, now, rng)
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc)
                best_a = np.clip(a1, 0.0, 1000.0).copy()

    # -------------------------
    # Final cleanup and verification
    # -------------------------
    if best_a is None:
        n_out = 64
        best_a = np.ones(n_out, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        best_a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        best_a = normalize_sequence(best_a, len(best_a))

    final_score = sequence_score(best_a, len(best_a))
    if final_score < best_score:
        best_score = float(final_score)

    out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, float(v)))) for v in best_a]
    if sum(out) <= 0.01:
        out = [1.0] * 64

    verified = best_score
    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        verified = best_score

    if np.isfinite(verified) and verified < best_score:
        best_score = verified

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
    print(f"[final] internal={best_score:.8f}, verified={verified:.8f}, n={len(out)}, time={elapsed:.1f}s")

    return out
```
